tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsExtendedForAttractionsAndTicketInfo::test_prompt_mentions_attractions_and_ticket_info[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue and extract the\nevent it announces.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve a relative expression\nyourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta", "15/08" and "22h" must all\nbe returned as the literal text you saw, character for character (aside from\ntrimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic resolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns\nthat text into a real date, anchored to the post's own timestamp. Guessing or\ncomputing a date yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere, leave the corresponding field null.\nNever invent one.\n\n## Fields to extract\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this post announces a real,\n  identifiable event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences:\n{"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n "recurrence_text": null,\n "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": null,\n "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n] PASSED [ 64%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsExtendedForAttractionsAndTicketInfo::test_prompt_mentions_attractions_and_ticket_info[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue or city-listings\naccount. The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic\nresolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns that text into a real date, anchored to the\npost's own timestamp, separately for each event. Guessing or computing a\ndate yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere for a given event, leave the\ncorresponding field null for that event. Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. If an event names no venue, leave\n`location_text` null for THAT event \u2014 never reuse or guess another event's\nvenue for it.\n\n## Fields to extract, PER EVENT\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: THIS event's own venue/address text, or null if none stated\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 65%]

A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this post announces a real,\n  identifiable event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences:\n{"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n "recurrence_text": null,\n "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": null,\n "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n] PASSED [ 65%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateKindAndItsPrecedence::test_prompt_names_every_kind[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue or city-listings\naccount. The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic\nresolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns that text into a real date, anchored to the\npost's own timestamp, separately for each event. Guessing or computing a\ndate yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere for a given event, leave the\ncorresponding field null for that event. Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. If an event names no venue, leave\n`location_text` null for THAT event \u2014 never reuse or guess another event's\nvenue for it.\n\n## Fields to extract, PER EVENT\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: THIS event's own venue/address text, or null if none stated\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 65%]

Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 65%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateKindAndItsPrecedence::test_prompt_puts_event_first_deliberately[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue and extract the\nevent it announces.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve a relative expression\nyourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta", "15/08" and "22h" must all\nbe returned as the literal text you saw, character for character (aside from\ntrimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic resolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns\nthat text into a real date, anchored to the post's own timestamp. Guessing or\ncomputing a date yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere, leave the corresponding field null.\nNever invent one.\n\n## Fields to extract\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this post announces a real,\n  identifiable event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences:\n{"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n "recurrence_text": null,\n "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": null,\n "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n] PASSED [ 65%]

The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic\nresolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns that text into a real date, anchored to the\npost's own timestamp, separately for each event. Guessing or computing a\ndate yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere for a given event, leave the\ncorresponding field null for that event. Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. If an event names no venue, leave\n`location_text` null for THAT event \u2014 never reuse or guess another event's\nvenue for it.\n\n## Fields to extract, PER EVENT\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: THIS event's own venue/address text, or null if none stated\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 65%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateCategoryAndItsVocabulary::test_prompt_mentions_category[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue and extract the\nevent it announces.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve a relative expression\nyourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta", "15/08" and "22h" must all\nbe returned as the literal text you saw, character for character (aside from\ntrimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic resolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns\nthat text into a real date, anchored to the post's own timestamp. Guessing or\ncomputing a date yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere, leave the corresponding field null.\nNever invent one.\n\n## Fields to extract\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateCategoryAndItsVocabulary::test_prompt_states_the_seeded_category_vocabulary[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue or city-listings\naccount. The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic\nresolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns that text into a real date, anchored to the\npost's own timestamp, separately for each event. Guessing or computing a\ndate yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere for a given event, leave the\ncorresponding field null for that event. Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. If an event names no venue, leave\n`location_text` null for THAT event \u2014 never reuse or guess another event's\nvenue for it.\n\n## Fields to extract, PER EVENT\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: THIS event's own venue/address text, or null if none stated\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 65%]

Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve a relative expression\nyourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta", "15/08" and "22h" must all\nbe returned as the literal text you saw, character for character (aside from\ntrimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic resolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns\nthat text into a real date, anchored to the post's own timestamp. Guessing or\ncomputing a date yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere, leave the corresponding field null.\nNever invent one.\n\n## Fields to extract\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this post announces a real,\n  identifiable event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences:\n{"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n "recurrence_text": null,\n "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": null,\n "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n] PASSED [ 65%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateDateInterpretation::test_prompt_mentions_date_interpretation[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue or city-listings\naccount. The post may announce ONE event or SEVERAL \u2014 a daily roundup\nnaming several different parties at several different venues is common.\nExtract EVERY distinct event the post announces, however many there are.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption, for EACH event independently. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve\na relative expression yourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta",\n"15/08" and "22h" must all be returned as the literal text you saw, character\nfor character (aside from trimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic\nresolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns that text into a real date, anchored to the\npost's own timestamp, separately for each event. Guessing or computing a\ndate yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere for a given event, leave the\ncorresponding field null for that event. Never invent one.\n\n## Critical rule about venues\nEach event may be at a DIFFERENT venue. Copy each event's own venue/address\ntext into ITS OWN `location_text`. If an event names no venue, leave\n`location_text` null for THAT event \u2014 never reuse or guess another event's\nvenue for it.\n\n## Fields to extract, PER EVENT\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: THIS event's own venue/address text, or null if none stated\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this is a real, identifiable\n  event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, wrapping every event in an\n"events" array (a single-event post still returns a list of one):\n{"events": [\n  {"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n   "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n   "recurrence_text": null,\n   "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n   "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": "Venue A",\n   "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n]}\n] PASSED [ 66%]
tests/test_multi_event_extraction.py::TestBothPromptsStateDateInterpretation::test_prompt_states_it_is_optional_and_never_a_computed_date[## Role\nYou read a single Instagram post (caption + one flyer/event photo, when a\nphoto is attached) from a Brazilian bar/club/event venue and extract the\nevent it announces.\n\n## Critical rule about dates and times\nCopy date and time EXACTLY AS WRITTEN OR SPOKEN on the flyer or in the\ncaption. Do NOT compute, normalize, or resolve a relative expression\nyourself \u2014 "amanh\xe3", "este s\xe1bado", "toda quinta", "15/08" and "22h" must all\nbe returned as the literal text you saw, character for character (aside from\ntrimming whitespace). A downstream deterministic resolver \u2014 not you \u2014 turns\nthat text into a real date, anchored to the post's own timestamp. Guessing or\ncomputing a date yourself here would be invisible and wrong.\n\nIf no date or time appears anywhere, leave the corresponding field null.\nNever invent one.\n\n## Fields to extract\n- kind: what this post actually IS \u2014 exactly one of:\n    - "event": a happening at a time \u2014 show, party, DJ night, live music\n    - "promotion": an offer or price advantage \u2014 happy hour, birthday freebie\n    - "menu": a dish or menu announcement, including a daily special\n    - "food": food or drink imagery with no offer and no event\n    - "other": anything else \u2014 staff, decor, hiring, closure notices,\n      birthday/anniversary greetings, thank-you or recap posts about a\n      night that already happened\n  Decide with this PRECEDENCE, in order, and stop at the FIRST that applies:\n  a happening with a date or recurring schedule -> "event"; else an offer or\n  price advantage -> "promotion"; else a named dish or menu -> "menu"; else\n  food or drink imagery -> "food"; else "other". This puts "event" FIRST on\n  purpose: a genuine event advertised alongside a drinks offer is still an\n  event, never a promotion.\n  BEFORE applying that precedence, ask whether the post actually ANNOUNCES\n  something a reader could attend \u2014 not merely whether it MENTIONS an\n  event, a venue, a date, or a lineup. A post that congratulates, thanks,\n  recaps, or reports on something is "other", even when it names a real\n  event, a real venue, a real date, and a real lineup: "Parab\xe9ns pelos seus\n  31 anos! Feliz anivers\xe1rio!" is a birthday greeting, not a party\n  announcement, even if it states a date and tags a venue. A caption\n  thanking everyone who came out last Friday, naming the acts that played,\n  is a RECAP of a night already over, not an invitation to a future one \u2014\n  "other", not "event", no matter how much detail it includes about what\n  already happened. The test is always "does this announce something\n  attendable", never "does this post contain event-shaped words".\n  A post with no photo attached still gets a kind, judged from the caption\n  alone. This field is REQUIRED \u2014 always answer with one of the five values\n  above, never omit it.\n- title: the event's name/headline\n- description: any additional descriptive text (optional)\n- date_text: the raw date expression exactly as printed/spoken, or null\n- time_text: the raw time expression exactly as printed/spoken (may be a\n  range like "22h \xe0s 04h"), or null\n- date_interpretation: OPTIONAL. Only when `date_text` is a shape you are\n  not confident a simple parser can read on its own (a bare "\xc9 HOJE", a\n  weekday paired with a day number like "Quinta (02)") \u2014 null when\n  `date_text` is already a plain, ordinary date ("15/08", "5 de setembro").\n  NEVER a computed or absolute date \u2014 only the literal pieces you read off\n  the text, tagged by shape:\n    {"kind": "relative", "relative": "hoje"} or {"relative": "amanha"}\n    {"kind": "day_month", "day": 6, "month": 2}\n    {"kind": "day_month_year", "day": 6, "month": 2, "year": 2027}\n    {"kind": "weekday", "weekday": "quinta"}\n    {"kind": "weekday_day", "weekday": "quinta", "day": 2}\n    {"kind": "range", "range_first_day": 6, "range_last_day": 9, "month": 2}\n  `weekday` is one of: segunda, ter\xe7a, quarta, quinta, sexta, s\xe1bado,\n  domingo. `month` is the numeral 1-12. Omit any field this shape does not\n  use rather than guessing a value for it.\n- is_recurring: true only if the text explicitly states a recurring\n  cadence ("toda quinta", "todo s\xe1bado", "semanalmente")\n- recurrence_text: the raw recurrence phrase when is_recurring, else null\n- attractions: array of every DJ, live act or performer named for this\n  event, each as an object \u2014 NOT a separate "lineup" field; the performer\n  list is derived from this array, so do not repeat it as its own field:\n    - name: the act/DJ name or handle, exactly as written\n    - type: "dj" for a DJ set, "live" for a live band/show, "other" when the\n      text does not say which\n    - stage: the stage/pista/room this act plays, exactly as written, or\n      null when the flyer names only one stage or none at all\n    - styles: array of this act's musical styles, using ONLY these labels:\n      Pagode, Samba, Sertanejo, Funk, Eletr\xf4nica, Techno, House, Pop, Rock, Indie, Rap / Trap, MPB, Reggaeton, Forr\xf3, Jazz, M\xfasica ambiente, Brega, Frevo. Omit any style not in this list. Empty array if no\n      style is stated.\n  Empty array if the post names no acts at all.\n- ticket_info: any purchase reference that is NOT itself a URL \u2014 a bare\n  emoji ticket label, "link na bio", "ingressos na bilheteria", a WhatsApp\n  number, an @handle, a lote/deadline note \u2014 copied verbatim. Populate this\n  ALONGSIDE ticket_url when the caption states both a link and a note;\n  neither suppresses the other. Null when nothing about buying or entry is\n  stated.\n- ticket_url: a ticketing link if present, else null\n- price_text: price/cover text exactly as stated (e.g. "R$30 antecipado"),\n  else null\n- location_text: any venue/address text named in the post (do not resolve\n  it, just copy it), else null\n- category: a short label for what this is \u2014 a music style, a theme, a\n  kind of offer, in a few words. PREFER one of these when it genuinely\n  fits: live music, DJ / club night, samba / pagode, forr\xf3, rock, MPB, jazz, sertanejo, funk, karaoke, comedy, quiz / trivia, kids / family, workshop, food festival, tasting, sports screening, party. If nothing on that list fits, answer freely with your\n  own short label rather than forcing a bad match \u2014 do not stretch a\n  listed word to cover something it does not really describe. Null only\n  when the post gives no basis for a category at all.\n- confidence: your own 0.0-1.0 confidence that this post announces a real,\n  identifiable event\n\n## Output\nReply with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences:\n{"kind": "event", "title": "...", "description": null, "date_text": "15/08",\n "time_text": "22h", "date_interpretation": null, "is_recurring": false,\n "recurrence_text": null,\n "attractions": [{"name": "DJ X", "type": "dj", "stage": null, "styles": ["House"]}],\n "ticket_url": null, "ticket_info": null, "price_text": "R$30", "location_text": null,\n "category": "rock", "confidence": 0.85}\n] PASSED [ 66%]
